# Control-plane production readiness — implementation tracker

This is the executable follow-up to the current account-control-plane implementation, not a claim that all capabilities are deployed. Existing APIs, source authority boundaries and `AGENTS.md` gates remain load-bearing. Work is delivered in verified increments; external source contracts and production exercises are separate acceptance gates.

## First increment design

### Request fingerprints

- Persist a versioned SHA-256 digest with the authoritative Operation/Sync row in its existing audited transaction.
- Bind verified actor/tenant/issuer/subject/client, target, command type and normalized payload. Dataset selections are sets; workflow steps retain their order.
- Check the digest on normal replay and on the unique-insert race recovery path. Return a sanitized dotted conflict code for a changed request.
- Historical rows have no verified original client binding: reject key reuse conservatively and retain their GET/list/reconciliation paths. Never silently generate a second command.
- Durable resource creation bypasses the generic HTTP response cache so cached responses cannot hide payload conflicts or changed authorization. Legacy response caching remains compatible.

### Data lifecycle barrier

- Serialize writes with the authoritative account row, check disposition while locked, and keep one lock order with erase.
- Valid late source events must have an explicit durable disposition; no poison-event success acknowledgements and no unbounded retries.
- An erased account cannot be restored by source version, summary, direct upsert, projection replay or a stale replica.
- Preserve existing download rejection; separately cover export capture/storage races and approved minimal retention/Legal Hold.

### Export completeness

- Honor the normalized submitted dataset selection, never an implicit all-snapshot read.
- Traverse source and membership keyset pages with item/byte/cursor bounds; fail rather than silently truncate.
- Report selected scope, counts, per-source freshness and explicit completeness. This is a local projection export, not a global source snapshot or Vault blob export.

## Verification log

- Baseline: `make test` passed before implementation (Go 1.26.8).
- Docker daemon is available for real integration fixtures. Availability does not prove image retrieval: Increment 9's actual MinIO fixture failed to pull the shipped pinned image.
- The existing uncommitted `AGENTS.md` update is preserved; it is not part of a rollback or cleanup.
- No live source credentials/deployments have been supplied. Real source and browser production acceptance cannot be inferred from repository tests.

### Increment 1 — implemented behavior

- Migration `000071` adds internal versioned request digests; startup and `migrate verify` validate watermark and actual Operation/Sync columns. Ordinary and insert-race receipt reuse reject changed/unbound intents and reauthorize.
- Local account locks fence profile, Summary, source/membership/snapshot and omission writes. Late events retain a redacted lifecycle quarantine/receipt and audit fact, not source payload or replay content. Summary/Event/direct updates and erase now append the snapshot replication change log.
- Account exports select persisted datasets, traverse source-scoped reference pages, reject stalled cursors and enforce 10,000-reference/byte budgets. Their manifest reports scope/missing coverage/counts and non-global-point-in-time semantics. All finish/reload/GET/list paths filter account-export content; only encrypted TTL artifacts hold content. A disposition race discards the artifact, and unknown deletion retains its key/TTL and reconciles disposal instead of regenerating an export.
- Compose uses `/app/migrate`, shares mandatory claims/AuthZ/issuer wiring and exact browser CORS, and provides `deployments/.env.example`. README now describes actual identity/source prerequisites, migration compatibility and retry/export contracts.
- Web uses the verified `/me` account/tenant header, remounts context and cancels pending reads when identity changes, and preserves in-memory tokens/deadline/body bounds. Sync/Export requires explicit selection; ambiguous creation keys are bounded and scoped by deployment/issuer/client/tenant/account in sessionStorage (memory fallback if blocked). No write is automatically retried.
- Job/Operation detail links accept only UUID resources and retain legacy page hashes. Metadata GET polling waits for each response, backs off, pauses hidden tabs/actions/errors, stops at terminal/unknown status and caps requests/wall time. Manual status refresh never runs/reconciles a command.

### Increment 2 — capability eligibility and local freshness

- `GET /v1/me/capabilities` requires verified, consistent actor/issuer/subject/tenant/client context and resolves only that identity's account. Production reads account/snapshot metadata without profile, reference or projection content. The response is `no-store` and separates registration, configuration enablement, self/global eligibility, availability and local freshness.
- Global checks use the existing Snaplink authority, with a fixed permission allow-list, four workers and a shared three-second deadline. Denials remain denied; dependency errors produce redacted unknown/partial states, never a local-role fallback or grant. Snaplink Check has no policy-version contract: `authorization_version: unsupported` is explicit. Source resource authorization is always unknown; this report does not fan out to sources or assert their health.
- Web validates the versioned report at runtime and checks issuer/client scope. Menus/deep links fail closed when eligibility is unavailable; Sync/Export buttons honor enablement. Unconfigured sources cannot create a new sync but can be explicitly selected to export existing local projections. The overview separately counts registered/enabled/local-read-eligible/fresh datasets and displays versions/timestamps. Reports are generated-time guidance, never command authorization or causal watermarks.
- OpenAPI, the bidirectional route manifest/fully-wired router fixture, client contract and Web guide include the new endpoint and its limits.
- PASS: complete backend unit tests, lint/strict lint, all backend static gates, integration compilation/vet and build. PASS: Web 33 Vitest files / 127 tests, lint, types, formatting and build. PASS: actual PostgreSQL metadata-query scope/content test (`/tmp/aero-id-capability-metadata-integration.log`).
- NOT PASS: another CI-environment full integration attempt encountered Docker container-create/delete deadlines and again hit the outer 20-minute timeout before repository completion (`/tmp/aero-id-capability-full-integration.log`). It also exposed an omitted capability registrar in the OpenAPI test fixture; that fixture/manifest were fixed, and the complete HTTP unit suite subsequently passed. This is not a successful full integration run; no host-wide container cleanup or mock substitution was performed.
- R1 command intents/current-version execution, distributed lifecycle/artifact correctness, Callback/exception remediation and the remaining R3/R4 work are not implemented by this increment. Full startup, browser/source, WORM and WAN/fencing acceptance remain separate blocked gates.

### Increment 3 — original-artifact recovery and local download fencing

- Deterministic storage writes checkpoint the original key, plaintext digest/size, metadata-only result and TTL before Put. Checkpoint uncertainty does not initiate a storage write. A lost Put acknowledgement resumes by reading that original object; NotFound stays unknown until disposal, never authorizing regeneration or TTL extension. Checkpoints reject changed keys/digests/sizes and retain original result metadata.
- Recovery/download validates durable artifact identity, request scope, content digest and identical job/artifact expiry. JSON decoding preserves large integer versions and rejects trailing content; plaintext budgets account separately for encryption overhead. Account and audit export GET/list/finish results omit embedded historical content without claiming persisted history is cleaned.
- Final export publication, final local download admission and erase serialize account → job. Erase additionally validates job/account/tenant scope. One-time pointer consumption and successful-read audit commit atomically; failed audit rolls consumption back. Current download permission is checked again after object retrieval. Object deletion remains outside the transaction, with its checkpoint retained until confirmed cleanup. Already-admitted/sent bytes may arrive after erase and cannot be recalled.
- Expiry filtering precedes cleanup limits; unknown jobs, consumed pointers awaiting disposal and lease-expired running artifacts remain discoverable. This closes page starvation by earlier unexpired jobs, not late remote-write orphaning, deployment object-lifecycle proof, historical retention/Legal Hold or cross-region erasure.
- `cmd/preflight` now reuses startup's JWKS constructor and claims-capability guard without database/application wiring or a Snaplink JWKS fetch. Tests cover missing/invalid config, parity and no network calls. Source trust still uses its existing separate preflight; live login/AuthZ/issuer/client registration acceptance remains pending.
- PASS: complete backend unit suite (`/tmp/aero-id-export-preflight-full-unit.log`); lint/strict lint, architecture/size/audit/load/migration/startup/composition/Proto/Kubernetes gates, integration compile/vet and build (`/tmp/aero-id-export-recovery-final-gates.log`, reconfirmed together in `/tmp/aero-id-export-preflight-done-gates.log`). PASS: PostgreSQL publication/erase/download race, concurrent one-time admission, original checkpoint TTL/key and real transactional audit rollback (`/tmp/aero-id-export-fence-postgres-verified.log`). PASS: PostgreSQL expiry cleanup including unknown/consumed artifacts and limit starvation (`/tmp/aero-id-export-recovery-postgres-rerun.log`, first test).
- PASS: the existing PostgreSQL duplicate-recovery/lease-fence regression with the correctly scoped export checkpoint (`/tmp/aero-id-checkpoint-legacy-postgres-final.log`).
- NOT PASS: targeted grouped fixtures encountered Docker create/delete deadlines and tool deadlines. A full CI-environment gate again hit the outer 20-minute timeout after container deadlines (`/tmp/aero-id-export-recovery-full.log`); no tests were skipped or replaced with mocks. The bounded-resource (`GOMAXPROCS=4`) rerun also encountered Docker create/delete deadlines and the outer timeout (`/tmp/aero-id-export-recovery-full-bounded.log`); reducing Go resources was an experiment, not a verified Docker fix. No host-wide container cleanup was performed.
- PASS: Web 33 Vitest files / 127 tests, lint, typecheck and formatting (`/tmp/aero-id-export-preflight-web.log`); the combined command hit a tool deadline during production build, so it is not reported as a successful `make web-check`. The production build passed separately (`/tmp/aero-id-export-preflight-web-build.log`). Real-browser/source E2E remains unverified.
- Corrected new fixture mistakes exposed by real execution (required ledger signing key and expiry stored in JSON result, not a nonexistent column); all five final fence subtests passed with the real configured ledger. Audit-export metadata tests use the actual `event_count`/`events` contract.
- R1 command intents/preview, account-level regional disposition/stale-writer fences, the remaining R2/R3/R4 implementation and independent source/browser/load/chaos/WORM acceptance are still open. This increment does not close those work items.

### Increment 4 — durable command preview and confirmed receipt admission

- Migration `000072` persists principal/client/tenant-scoped server plans, request fingerprints, confirmation digests, local preconditions, expiry and the original receipt. Startup and `migrate verify` validate schema version, column shapes and unique intent-key scope; the down migration refuses to discard any durable intent. Five-minute preview expiry is not retention authorization.
- HTTP Create/Get/Execute are wired through the composition root, with verified claims, `no-store`, strict 256 KiB JSON/32-step budgets and generic idempotency-cache bypass. Preview performs no source call or execution. Requests cannot override tenant or inject an execution plan. New workflow previews require explicit same-source scope; immutable plans preserve ordered steps and redacted normalized payloads.
- Execute reauthorizes and rebuilds only the stored plan under a shared three-second planning budget. It atomically checks directory → regional epoch → account → intent, current local snapshot metadata, version/confirmation and database-clock expiry, then creates the existing Operation/Sync receipt and both facts. Concurrent confirmed retries return one original receipt; audit failure rolls everything back. No new execution engine, automatic run or rollback was introduced. Snaplink policy version is unsupported; source ACL remains unknown and source resource authorization is still required.
- Operation preparation checks compensation effects and destructive scope as well as forward actions. Manual Run/Reconcile now recheck the effective stored plan after the management permission check. Delayed/background worker and per-effect authorization remain open; acceptance-time checks are not a distributed transaction with Snaplink.
- Web Sync/Export uses typed, runtime-validated server previews and explicit confirmation. Ambiguous prepare/execute results preserve the original intent/key/confirmation, never automatically posting or changing keys. Preview keys use a separate scope from old direct-create keys; unresolved legacy requests block new previews. Expiry disables confirmation but does not silently renew an intent. Operation/admin/erase preview wiring, expired-intent disposition UX and real browser acceptance remain open.
- PASS: complete backend unit suite, lint/strict lint, all architecture/size/audit/load/migration/startup/composition/Proto/Kubernetes gates, integration compile/vet and build (`/tmp/aero-id-command-intent-done-gates.log`). PASS: actual PostgreSQL concurrent receipt, erase-first, delayed-lock expiry, disposition/region epoch change, schema-shape and transactional audit rollback tests (`/tmp/aero-id-command-intent-schema-postgres-verified.log`, seven subtests). The first attempts exposed missing fixture directory setup/table names and a wrong not-found expectation; these were corrected, not replaced with mocks.
- PASS: full `make web-check`, 35 Vitest files / 133 tests, lint, typecheck, formatting and production build (`/tmp/aero-id-command-intent-web-verified.log`). A prior fixture omitted required typed metadata; that fixture was corrected before the final full pass.
- NOT PASS: the full CI-environment integration rerun reached the existing 20-minute outer timeout (`/tmp/aero-id-command-intent-full-integration.log`, Make status 2 / target Error 124). Audit replay, composition-root app, event, idempotency, outbox and migration packages passed, but the repository package never reported a result before termination. This log alone does not establish whether its unreported work was slow Docker infrastructure or a code failure. No host-wide container cleanup, fixture skips or real-source acceptance claims; full-suite fixture cleanup cannot be inferred after external termination.
- Cross-region lifecycle, late Put orphan/lifecycle/retention evidence and the remaining R2/R3/R4 work stay open. Real Snaplink/source, browser, WORM, WAN, canary and old-writer acceptance remain independently blocked.

### Increment 5 — Operation continuation authorization and unknown-attempt safety

- Production Operation execution now uses the Snaplink checker directly (no legacy fallback), checking the durable initiator's root/action grants before claims and again before forward, compensation and status-query calls under a shared three-second admission deadline. Current target tenant/account/home/disposition are checked without fabricating JWT identity or restoring user credentials. Ownership/preview eligibility alone is not a delayed-worker grant; source enablement must verify the initiator's grants on the configured AuthZ client. Manual operators must pass their existing management checks and cannot lend their privileges to the initiator.
- Pre-effect local admission verifies the exact lease owner/kind and PostgreSQL clock, account disposition and directory/region epoch. These point checks are not atomic with Snaplink/source requests or a recall mechanism; source resource authorization and fencing remain necessary. Snaplink policy version remains unsupported.
- Denials/outages commit redacted `operation.execution.blocked` metadata/facts without discarding outcomes, changing terminality or creating a new command. Each blocking execution is anchored to its fresh durable fact ID, not the resource. Background queries pace these conditions locally for one minute and a blocked item does not stop later eligible items; audit uncertainty still propagates. A claimed action may return to pending only before the runner is called; original unknown/status-query claims remain unknown with their original IDs/results.
- Fixed the expired-forward transaction sentinel path: it now commits the unknown transition and expiry fact before returning `ErrOperationStepUnknown`, instead of rolling the mutation back. Expired compensation likewise becomes audited unknown, never a new compensation command. Reverse compensation claims fence subsequent effects behind pending/running/unknown predecessors. Unknown forward effects are not rollback authorization; deferred pending compensation remains discoverable after a known failure. Attempt/event-ID shapes are independently golden-pinned without widening the fresh-ID allowlist.
- PASS: complete backend unit tests, vet/lint/strict, all architecture/size/audit/load/migration/startup/composition/Proto/Kubernetes gates, integration compile/vet and build (`/tmp/aero-id-operation-execution-done-gates-verified.log`). New deterministic tests cover denial/outage/missing and typed-nil authority, no legacy fallback, account/tenant/home/disposition mismatch, malformed scopes, after-claim/between-step revocation, compensation permission, unknown preservation, lease loss, bounded late grants, initiator versus operator scope, distinct audit execution IDs, ledger failure and continued worker batches.
- PASS: real PostgreSQL eight-subtest fixture (expiry commit/no reissue, compensation expiry/barrier, audited deferral/discovery, paced unknown preservation, directory/disposition fencing, atomic audit rollback and manual-worker lease competition). `/tmp/aero-id-operation-execution-fences-verified.log` is the final isolated successful package run (11.39 seconds including explicit container cleanup). The grouped run also passed this fixture and the forward audit and standalone service audit regressions but FAILED a separate compensation fixture's Docker deletion deadline (`/tmp/aero-id-operation-execution-postgres-verified.log`). That compensation regression subsequently passed in isolation including cleanup (`/tmp/aero-id-operation-execution-compensation-regression.log`, 51.66 seconds); this does not turn the earlier grouped command into a pass. PASS: new service race-detector subset (`/tmp/aero-id-operation-execution-race.log`).
- NOT PASS: the full CI-environment gate again hit its existing 20-minute outer timeout (`/tmp/aero-id-operation-execution-full-integration.log`, Make status 2 / Error 124). Earlier packages including app/event/outbox/migrations passed; repository did not report a result. Process inspection near timeout showed the repository binary had run about seven minutes of its allowed fifteen minutes; the target's whole-run budget expires first. This does not establish which tests or Docker operations were slow, or prove cleanup. The isolated, unfiltered repository diagnostic ultimately FAILED its fifteen-minute package timeout (`/tmp/aero-id-operation-execution-repository-diagnostic.jsonl`, status 1). It started 158 tests, reported 157 passes and no individual test failure before the package panic; `TestAuditWiringMatrixFileShareGrantMulti` had been active for 22 seconds, with its stack in the real Docker container-removal cleanup. This establishes cumulative budget exhaustion during cleanup, not a successful package, proof that this particular business test hung, or proof that all fixtures were cleaned. No unrelated containers or fixtures were removed/skipped. Web was not changed in this backend increment; its last complete pass remains Increment 4, not new browser evidence.
- Remaining R1 work: Sync/Export/Erase/audit-job continuation authorization, real authority/resource-ACL/lease-loss/WAN acceptance, region-wide lifecycle/old-writer and artifact/storage/retention proof. R1-04 remains partial; other Web actions, R2/R3/R4 and external acceptance remain open. Schema remains `000072`; this increment adds no migration or source API.

### Increment 6 — Sync continuation authority, local lease admission and original-outcome preservation

- Wired a separate direct Snaplink execution checker for Sync/Reconcile/Erase/Export/audit-export jobs; enqueue's ownership and legacy checker cannot renew a worker grant. After claim and before each source/status call, projection application/replay, content page, artifact Put/recovery read and publication, the stored initiator's current permission and tenant/account/home/disposition are rechecked under a shared three-second admission deadline. Audit export also requires `audit.read` per page. Operators cannot lend privileges; no JWT/token fabrication, policy version or source ACL is invented.
- Local admission checks exact lease token/kind, database-clock deadline and directory/region epoch. Recovery row locking also rechecks the database clock after waiting. Audited `sync.execution.blocked` deferral preserves original source operation IDs/results, summary replays and metadata-only artifact key/digest/TTL. Only untouched execute claims without recovery state return pending; started/reconcile claims remain unknown. Pending/unknown eligibility is paced locally for a minute and safely blocked claims do not starve following eligible work. Lease loss or uncertain deferral/audit does not rewrite another owner's state or authorize a new command.
- Fixed Sync expired-execute sentinel rollback: unknown and `sync.execution.expired` audit commit before returning `ErrSyncJobUnknown`. Regional/manual worker paths share claim logic and database-clock deadlines. The fresh fact ID distinguishes blocking executions; expiry is durable-attempt-scoped, with four independent UUIDv5/NUL-separated goldens and no widened random-EventID allowlist.
- Source success before revocation keeps its durable summary/outcome and awaits authorized original recovery; known erase success is not sent again. `not_accepted` status does not lend permission to resend: current erase admission must pass again with the same key. Missing summary recovery capability stops rather than issuing a new source read. Recovery validates resolved replay binding/scope too. Initial audit-export requests with normalized datasets but no continuation field now correctly start at the first page; present malformed continuation fields still fail closed.

### Increment 7 — Web account/Operation confirmation, scoped recovery and current-action guidance

- Self Sync/Export/Erase, admin account tasks and Operation creation now share the server-owned preview/confirmation path. Scope must be explicitly selected; Operation forward/compensation steps retain order and same-source scopes. Confirmation accepts the server plan and only its original Job/Operation receipt, never the old direct-create APIs or an automatic Run. Destructive confirmation displays the exact target; source erase enablement and resource ACL remain server/source checks, not inferred from summary enablement.
- Shared recovery persists at most 16 bounded caller/deployment-scoped UUID/hash pointers before the first POST, without tokens, payloads, source/export content or confirmation input. Unknown preparation requires the original parameters/key; a known ID uses GET only. Immutable metadata, target/caller/kind and receipt are checked, including the authoritative PostgreSQL microsecond lifetime precision of Create versus Get/execute; a one-microsecond expiry change is rejected. Corrupt/over-budget state fails closed and cannot expose raw JSON parser content. Disabled storage falls back to memory without claiming refresh recovery. Late replies cannot clear another intent's key, and confirmed account erase retains its receipt pointer across refresh.
- Sync/Export/Admin/Erase share one account-task channel for the same target. Legacy direct-create/unbound erase keys block new previews; only an exact original server-preview scope/selection/key may resume. Previous Operation/admin keys that existed only in memory cannot be discovered after refresh: operators must check original lists/receipts before rollout. Expiry/conflict offers original-intent/receipt GET recovery, not renewal, abandonment, key deletion, a new command or proof that an old in-flight execute cannot commit. Explicit disposition/new-intent policy and hold-aware intent retention remain unimplemented.
- Current capability guidance now refreshes with coalesced metadata GETs, explicit refresh, focus/visibility and 60-second visible-window pacing. While checking, on failure or beyond the local 120-second hint budget, old grants do not authorize new commands. Async preview hashing rechecks guidance before POST. Missing providers or mismatched account/tenant/issuer/client fail closed. Only the exact already-admitted page/detail can remain visible during unknown refresh; a new menu/deep link cannot borrow an old report. Explicit current read denial still applies. This is local UI guidance, not a policy version, global limiter or execution credential; backend/worker/source reauthorization remains necessary.
- Existing Run/Reconcile/one-time download actions check the actual root/forward/compensation/job grants at click time and continue the original receipt/key. Wrong-target results cannot replace details or clear reconciliation keys. Authorization errors request only a capability GET, not a write retry. Added read AbortSignals, pagination/unmount guards and stale-completion protection. This gating covers account tasks and Operation, not every remaining Profile/governance write.

### Increment 8 — explicit unexecuted-intent withdrawal and original-key recovery

- Added migration `000073`, mandatory at startup/`migrate verify`, with nullable database-clock `abandoned_at`, exact prepared-v1/executed-v2/abandoned-v2 constraints and an immutable terminal UPDATE guard. The shape gate checks actual columns, validated CHECK text and enabled trigger/function definition, not only a watermark. Down refuses any abandonment proof, even after expiry; no physical purge, TTL renewal or Legal Hold release is introduced. Plan/key/expiry/receipt facts remain retained. The local database guard rejects older writers reopening a terminal intent; global/WAN old-writer acceptance remains external.
- `POST /v1/command-intents/:id/abandon` is wired through the existing composition root/HTTP service/repository. It requires the verified original actor/issuer/subject/client/tenant and original version/digest, uses a three-second local budget and directory → epoch → account → intent locks, and checks the current regional writer/fence. It does not replan, check renewed generation grants, read content or call sources: withdrawing one's own unsent preview creates no command effect. Expired/stale/erased preparations can be closed, but cannot borrow another principal's privileges. New preparation/execution still requires current action/source authorization.
- Execute and withdrawal have one local terminal winner. Withdrawal first commits `abandoned` v2 plus `command.intent.abandoned` in one transaction, permanently refusing late Execute. Execute first returns its original executed Job/Operation receipt, never pretending to cancel/roll back unknown or admitted effects. Identical retries return the retained result without another mutation/fact. Failed audit rolls back or reports persistence uncertainty. Receipt/disposition response versions are computed before GORM mutates the update model, and PostgreSQL tests assert version 2 explicitly.
- `GET /v1/command-intents/by-key` is an original-caller scoped metadata lookup with the original key in a header, never a preparation POST. `data.intent` plus the SHA-256 key digest binds the lookup without echoing the key. NotFound/outage does not authorize a new key or prove an older preparation cannot still commit. HTTP strict/no-store envelopes, generic cache bypass and OpenAPI/bidirectional route manifest are updated (44 documented + 22 previously-undocumented = 66 operations); no source API, new package or execution engine.
- All four Web surfaces offer explicit withdrawal by entering the exact original intent ID, separately from execute confirmation. Confirmed closure retains UUID/hash/original-key pointers; unknown closure preserves input/key and supports GET recovery. Executed erase pointers cannot be treated as abandoned. No automatic withdrawal, new preview, Run or source rollback. An explicit next preparation re-reads all relevant retained closures under one 15-second overall GET budget and rechecks current guidance before generating a key. Local disposition flags alone are not authority; prepared/NotFound/changed metadata/wrong scope/failure block new keys. Late prepared/receipt replies cannot regress a closed pointer, replace its ID/hash or clear another intent's key. The 16-pointer budget includes retained closures and is not server retention.

### Increment 9 — owned Callback operational reads and conservative Web recovery

- Added a separate `CallbackDeliveryReader` and content-free domain DTO: explicit SQL selects ID/event/action, local status/attempt/claim generation/expiry, fixed error/HTTP codes and timestamps. No payload, receiver body, endpoint, worker identity or ciphertext lookup; no post-load redaction as the security boundary. Owner + verified tenant + subscription + delivery scope is checked together, including administrators. Lists use descending UUID keysets/status filters/limit+1, default20/max100 (Web50), not creation time or offset; owned empty results are audited.
- Metadata and a fresh `callback.deliveries.read` / `callback.delivery.read` fact commit in the same transaction with a parent subscription SHARE fence against deletion/ownership changes. Audit failure exposes no result and rolls back the fact; every read uses its own persisted fact ID/EventID, never resource-wide dedup. Historical action/status/errors are copied into fixed safe metadata, not returned as arbitrary diagnostics or rewritten in authority rows. Worker settlement is independent: current rows are not attempt history, receiver completion receipts, a global snapshot or causal watermarks.
- HTTP list/detail no-store and gRPC `ListCallbackDeliveries`/`GetCallbackDelivery` share the read service/AppError mapper; OpenAPI, route manifest and pinned gRPC permission registry are synchronized. Current verified actor/issuer/subject/client/tenant consistency and direct Snaplink `callback.manage` are required under the service's three-second authority/read/audit budget; nil/typed-nil, denial, outage and late grants fail closed. The composition root preserves the exact `buildAuditStack` seam, attaching the checker before Start. Attachment is one-time; constructor-option attachment/read/Start freezes even missing configuration, so late/replacement setters panic and cannot change a live dependency. No session/policy-version proof or distributed authorization transaction is invented.
- Migration `000074` adds subscription/UUID and subscription/status/UUID keyset indexes. Semantic startup/intent minimum remains73; apply the complete shipped set and budget transactional index-build locks. The existing disposition rollback fixture explicitly steps from any newer additive migrations to73 before proving refusal to discard abandonment evidence; migration73 was not weakened/edited. No new command engine/source API, retention policy or authority state is introduced.
- `callback.read` is configuration-independent guidance backed by existing global `callback.manage`, not a new permission or write grant. Delivery pause/missing key does not hide owned recovery metadata; Web `callback.manage` guidance additionally requires an active configured delivery path. One current permission decision is shared across the two report features. Callback navigation now needs the new read feature; an older report without it fails closed rather than borrowing management eligibility.
- Web selects subscriptions → bounded status-filtered metadata → exact delivery detail, without hand-entered Delivery IDs or automatic POST/polling. Runtime validation checks UUIDs, exact fields, tenant/target, fixed status/error types, numbers/timestamps, UUID order/duplicates/cursor progress and budgets. GETs capture original identity scope, reject late identity/target changes and honor cancellation. Shared paging aborts on replacement/unmount and never merges late pages. Read errors do not grant replay. Only a validated dead_letter candidate without a future local lease has an explicit replay prompt; expired delivering/unknown/2xx cannot prove no remote effect. Registration/delete/replay entry and confirmation require current guidance; authority errors trigger only capability GET refresh.
- Unknown legacy registration keeps its original form/key and refuses changed parameters; accepted metadata must match original UUID/tenant/endpoint/event set before clearing the key or displaying a one-time Secret. Unknown replay retains its original in-memory key/confirmation (16-intent budget); only queued is accepted, never remote completion. Late writes/Secret cannot be adopted after identity change/unmount. These existing write APIs still use limited HTTP response-cache idempotency, not durable body/client/version-bound intent or status/lease CAS: cache loss/expiry/overflow and concurrent state changes remain unsafe to infer. In-memory keys do not guarantee refresh recovery; no automatic retry/new key, task cancellation or source rollback is added. Read state/NotFound/expiry are not new-command authority.

## Increment 10 — durable version-bound Callback replay admission

- Reuses CommandIntent preview/confirmation/original-key recovery/abandon and the existing signed dispatcher. Typed `callback_replay` binds principal/client/tenant/own canonical account and original subscription/delivery/event/revision/lease generation. It forbids datasets/workflow/payload mixing; preview and Execute use current direct Snaplink permission/configuration and a three-second request budget.
- Migration `000075` (now the semantic startup minimum) adds a positive all-writer revision trigger and guarded callback receipts. Startup verifies enabled exact trigger/function and actual receipt constraints, not a bookkeeping watermark. Existing/old reset and worker UPDATE paths advance revision. Downgrade cannot discard callback replay intents.
- Execute requires the exact original dead-letter row with no owner or lease timestamp; stale/changed/expired-leased rows do not qualify. Account/directory/writer and parent/child fences remain in place; DB time is rechecked after child-lock waits. Queue modification/generation/fresh replay fact/executed intent receipt commit atomically. Audit failure rolls back all. Original event/payload/response evidence/attempt are preserved without selecting content or credentials.
- Receipt kind `callback_replay` uses intent ID and means local queued only. Same confirmation returns its original durable receipt even after worker settlement/parent deletion. Unknown/expiry/NotFound do not authorize a replacement key; abandon cannot cancel an already-queued send or release Legal Hold. This is not a transaction with Snaplink/receiver, and already-admitted/in-flight effects still require receiver event deduplication.
- HTTP existing CommandIntent routes and typed OpenAPI/metadata gRPC revision are wired. Web metadata/client validators understand revision/plan/receipt and scoped pointer kind; **the page button still uses old compatibility replay**, not the new durable preview. Its actual workflow/recovery switch remains open. Old gRPC replay retains its non-durable contract.

## Increment 11 — actual Callback Web durable preview and recovery

- The page no longer invokes compatibility `replayDelivery`. A dedicated `CallbackReplayPreview` shares the existing CommandIntent hook/storage/dialog and exposes explicit prepare/confirm/original-ID or original-key GET recovery/abandon; no new engine/API. A canonical fixed-order plan binds original subscription/delivery/event/revision/lease generation, with empty datasets only for this typed kind. Client rejects extra callback-plan fields and a recovered tuple that differs from the original signature.
- Confirmation requires both exact original Delivery ID and Callback Event ID, never generic text/account ID. Candidate guidance requires positive safe-integer revision and no lease timestamp, including expired values; service still checks actual owner/parent/row/lease/config/current direct permission and local fences. The dialog shows original tuple, revision/generation and local queue-only/remote-unknown boundaries. Current guidance is checked after hashing/closure reads and before any new POST, as well as confirmation. Read errors, report refresh/revocation and old metadata never lend permission.
- Pointer persistence precedes preparation POST, and all refresh recovery is GET-only. Unknown prepare retains original tuple/key in the mounted page for a separate explicit same-parameters/key request; after refresh no tuple/payload is stored, so lookup uses only the original key. NotFound, prepared, expiry/stale/conflict or mismatch never permits another key, automatic retry, renewal, endpoint call or cancel. Pending previews block conflicting selections/actions; restoring the original intent is separate from unavailable subscription reads/current write qualification.
- Executed callback receipts stay in the bounded pointer store through refresh rather than being automatically discarded. Exact intent-ID operator acknowledgment only GET-verifies the original executed receipt/immutable hash and marks that pointer acknowledged, permitting another explicit selection; it does not queue anything or prove receiver completion/no prior effects. Before a later new preview all relevant abandoned/acknowledged pointers are re-read under one aggregate15-second GET budget, matching original caller/account/kind/ID/hash/terminal receipt; local flags/NotFound/prepared/outage/timeout do not suffice. The16-pointer cap counts terminal pointers; no eviction, purge/hold release or guaranteed retention cleanup.
- Late identity/scope/unmount responses cannot adopt or clear another view's pointer/selection; GETs abort, and terminal markers cannot regress. Execute-wins abandon retains the original local-queue receipt instead of pretending withdrawal cancelled delivery. Callback recovery does not borrow/migrate Sync/Erase legacy keys. Existing registration/delete and old HTTP/gRPC replay remain separate compatibility contracts; no old unknown replay outcome/key is upgraded. Original Callback Event ID dedup and real receiver authorization/acceptance remain required.

## Increment 12 — mandatory cluster-free manifest validation and valid quiesced renders

- Replaces optional ambient kubectl client dry-run in the static gate with mandatory validation in the existing `deployments/k8s` test package; no new production package/binary or controller. All five **actual shell-rendered** forms are retained and checked, including up (previously overwritten by version): steady14 + paused13 + up/down/version1 each =30 resources. No empty/partial/unsupported bundle, skipped schema or ambient tool/kubeconfig dependency. Fixture pause/action inputs are pinned even when unrelated options are inherited; the command and test have two/one-minute budgets. CI check-gates now explicitly provisions the project's Go toolchain rather than borrowing the runner version.
- Vendors the full nine v1.34.1 standalone-strict Kubernetes JSON schemas, byte-identical to upstream commit817113f21a2643311cbc3e992bb44ffe1c416c54, with license/provenance and reviewed SHA-256/full-GVK pins. This is a static baseline, not a claimed minimum/supported production cluster. A go.mod/go.sum-pinned JSON Schema engine (v6.0.2, deployment tests only) has no external URL/file loader; standard metaschemas are embedded. Missing/changed/oversized schema bytes fail instead of downloading or falling back. Duplicate YAML keys, unknown fields/full groups/versions/kinds (including List expansion), wrong types, malformed/empty inputs and byte/document budget excess are rejected. No miniature handwritten schema replaces upstream fields.

## Increment 16 — local Callback send admission without pretending to coordinate pause

- Runtime inspection confirms `delivery_paused` remains static per process boot; existing workers previously fenced only settlement, allowing an already queued stale claim to enter the sender before its result was refused. Add a metadata-only existence-check port to the existing repository and invoke it after local secret validation/before endpoint processing, then again immediately before the existing underlying HTTP transport. No new sender, workflow, route/RPC, principal, dynamic control API or migration; minimum remains75. All API/worker senders must upgrade, and configuration pause still needs every replica.
- Bind original delivery/subscription/event/action/attempt, worker/generation/exact lease deadline, PostgreSQL-clock expiry and current enabled/verified parent owner/tenant. No payload, response, endpoint or ciphertext SELECT. Each point check receives a3s context budget; the transport check also inherits the original HTTP request deadline. Context is not a hard interrupt for an implementation ignoring cancellation, but late return never authorizes dispatch. Missing/typed-nil ports, SQL errors, canceled contexts and late success fail closed, without a permissive fallback. These are local consistency points, not an additional Snaplink grant/session/policy-version, regional pause barrier, new global DNS/dispatch budget or distributed transaction with the receiver.
- Refusal does not settle, spend a remote attempt, renew/clear the lease, return a row to pending, rewrite diagnostics or create another key/event/delivery. Keep the original delivering row for existing lease recovery; earlier senders may already have effects. Fixed counter labels `send_ineligible`/`send_check_failed` distinguish failed eligibility from unavailable evidence, not durable attempt/exception history or backlog gauges. Preserve normal admitted-timeout behavior and original event/signature/header/body encoding. Request/response helpers are extracted to respect function limits, without lint exceptions.
- New deterministic late-success regression exposed net/http replacing a pre-transport gate timeout with its HTTP timeout error: preserve the explicit guarded refusal separately so it cannot be charged as a remote attempt. Conversely a timeout after entering the underlying transport remains an attempted send. Queue/bulkhead tests use channel barriers, not sleeps, to deny the second stale delivery after the first was admitted. No check can retract that first call/bytes; same-owner generation loss after admission still refuses settlement and requires original Event ID receiver deduplication.
- PASS complete backend gate including mandatory runner/relocation checks (`/tmp/aero-id-callback-send-gate-backend.log`,status0), expanded integration-tag strict lint across config/repository/service (`/tmp/aero-id-callback-send-gate-tagged-strict.log`,status0) and complete service/repository race (`/tmp/aero-id-callback-send-gate-race.log`,status0). ## Increment 17 — shared Callback network budget and owned, conservative egress

- Runtime inspection found context-free net.LookupIP before the request timer and connection-time DNS inside net/http's detached dial context. Reuse the existing Callback service/sender: registration starts one configured network-phase deadline before endpoint validation/challenge, delivery after bulkhead/local key validation and before the first lease check. Both checks inherit it with their existing3s caps; DNS, dial/TLS, headers and bounded body reads share the remainder. Default5s/max5m and earlier parent deadlines remain unchanged. Queue/decrypt/claim/settlement, governance publication and whole-drain/global limits are not newly covered. No new route/RPC/sender/authority, migration, credential or execution API; minimum remains75.
- Context-aware per-instance resolver/dial ports reject missing dependencies, empty/failed/malformed/over32-answer results and late success. Revalidate the entire connection-time answer and dial only validated literals; mixed answers fail rather than selecting their public members. Normalize mapped IPv4, reject shared/private/link-local/protocol/documentation/benchmark/reserved/transition ranges and restrict IPv6 to2000::/3 with special-use exclusions. Reject local/trailing-dot names, userinfo, insecure schemes and scoped addresses; retain no redirects/proxies and normal TLS identity verification. This conservative code-reviewed policy is not a full/live IANA registry or routing/receiver attestation; production DNS/CA/egress/NAT64/VPN acceptance remains required, not a production private-target bypass.
- Each per-call client disables connection reuse, caps response headers at64KiB and keeps the original body bounds. Bind both request-owner/transport cancellation during dial, retain the original owner after dial, apply its socket deadline and close the owned connection on cancellation during TLS/body work. Close late connections, response bodies and idle transports; preserve close errors on refused socket adoption. Contexts are not hard interrupts for noncooperating implementations/resolver-internal work. Returned-answer limits are not DNS-wire/OS-thread/memory/global concurrency limits, and bounded local calls cannot retract already-sent bytes/effects.
- Preserve the explicit pre-transport refusal marker: failed lease checks still do not settle/spend attempts/modify leases or diagnostics; destination DNS/validation/TLS/header/body failures keep the existing bounded delivery-attempt/retry/DLQ policy even where no HTTP bytes reached the receiver. Late challenge/body success is never adopted. Preserve original Event ID, HMAC/body/header encoding, signing secret and finite retries; no replacement key/event/delivery, new pause API or receiver-business receipt. README/OpenAPI and both Callback runbooks describe the budgets/policy versus compatibility and remote-effect boundaries; the general operations guide now prefers durable replay/current metadata and all-sender static pause. Runbook names the actual sender header X-Aero-Callback-Event-ID rather than the older inaccurate X-Aero-Event-ID note.
- PASS complete backend done gate plus mandatory runner/relocation contracts (`/tmp/aero-id-callback-network-backend.log`,status0); expanded integration-tag strict config/repository/service (`/tmp/aero-id-callback-network-tagged-strict.log`,status0). Complete service/repository race PASS1.607s/1.089s (`/tmp/aero-id-callback-network-race.log`); deterministic network/connection/send-gate race count10 PASS3.477s (`/tmp/aero-id-callback-network-race-first.log`). Coverage includes whole/malformed/oversized answers, mapped/special-use ranges, pre-DNS URL denial, rebinding/literal dial, late DNS/socket/challenge/body results, parent plus detached-transport cancellation, TLS handshake/body stall, header limit, HTTPS verification/redirect denial, owned cleanup and shared deadlines without renewal. Initial Register51-line lint finding was corrected by extracting the registration-verification helper, not raising limits/adding exceptions.
- PASS real PostgreSQL plus local HTTPS original-event lost-acknowledgment exercise and existing key-rotation/exact-lease/current-parent regressions under race (`/tmp/aero-id-callback-network-postgres-verified.log`,status0,19.291s). The controlled receiver applies the first signed Event ID but loses the response body; the same persisted row retries once and succeeds with the identical signed Event ID/secret, two requests and one deduplicated receiver effect. Explicit local CA/private-loopback test configuration is not production trust/receiver/WAN acceptance. An earlier non-race invocation also PASS19.609s, not a substitute for the complete gate. Each invocation's five exact owned fixture IDs independently inspect explicitly not-found, no manual removal or host-wide discovery; unknown/lost-create IDs are outside the proof.
- Actual unchanged original serial full gate **FAIL retained** (`/tmp/aero-id-callback-network-serial.log`,Make status2/Error124): p1/count1/all packages, original20-minute outer/15-minute package limits. Outer time expires, not a package deadline. Only37 of49 expected package terminals arrive (35 PASS/2 no-test-file skips),12 packages have no terminal including auditintegration/service/storage; no individual failed-test event is not a pass. Original185 inventory/digest is unchanged, but only168 started/167 passed (root132/audit35), one unfinished TestAuditWiringMatrixAdminBanBlockCommentMessageFileShare and17 never started; no duplicate/unexpected test. ## Increment 18 — 当前导出读取者的直接授权与审计双权限

- 按实际 `internal/app/services.go` 接线确认缺陷：生成审计导出已复查 `audit.export` + `audit.read`，下载却只使用可缺失/带兼容 fallback 的旧 permission checker、且遗漏 `audit.read`。先新增 deterministic 回归，服务端五类缺失/拒绝/故障及 Web 确认时撤销 `audit.read` 均复现失败（本轮日志前缀下 `increment18-regression-red.log` / `increment18-web-red.log`），不是仅静态推测。
- 复用已接线的直接 Snaplink checker；下载在查任务前校验完整、一致的 verified caller/issuer/subject/client/tenant，对象读取前及最终本地准入前均查询**当前读取者**，不是持久任务发起者。账户制品要求 `account.export`，审计制品同时要求 `audit.export` 和 `audit.read`；每个检查点内所有权限共用三秒 deadline，继承更早请求期限。nil/typed-nil、错误、取消和迟到允许 fail closed，无 ownership/原发起者/legacy fallback。当前有全局读取授权的同租户读取者仍可读原任务，不把生成授权转换成读取 grant。没有新增 route/RPC 契约、来源资源调用、凭据、迁移或平行执行引擎；schema75、request-binding minimum71 不变。
- 拒绝当前权限不消费一次性指针、不删原制品、不写成功读取事实、不换 key/重新 Put/续 TTL；对象 IO 后撤权也不返回内容。原 key/digest/size/TTL、持久检查点、账户 disposition/final download fence、消费+审计事务和删除不确定恢复保持原实现。每次成功读取仍有独立 fact ID，不改零 EventID 的已有语义或 anchors/goldens。三秒不是整个下载/存储期限，也不能硬中断忽略取消的依赖；迟到允许仍拒绝。这些是授权点检查，不是 Snaplink session/policy-version 事务，也不能召回已准入/发送字节，source ACL 与分布式擦除空白未关闭。
- Web 打开/最终确认审计下载均要求两个当前能力提示；权限未知/拒绝时暂停，不自动下载、Prepare 或生成新导出。对话框明确一次性消费/审计先于响应、丢回应/删除不确定可能无法重下。下载 handler 的成功/服务错误/无效 ID 响应明确 `no-store`，不声称覆盖上游代理或鉴权中间件。README/OpenAPI/client/Web/retention 指南同步；运维导出泄露章节纠正“签名导出 URL”和错误事实名，要求 owner 批准原制品/密钥处置，保留 Legal Hold、迟到写/跨区/在途字节未知边界，不建议直接修 job/checkpoint。

## Increment 19 — SSE replay 测试的有界同步与 race 回归

- 先完整读取本轮 `AGENTS.md` 和 tracker，核对 `internal/app/{new,services,handlers,grpc,workers}.go`、实际 Auth 路由与 `EventsHandler`：SSE 仍是兼容通知推送，接入同一个 Hub/notification repository，不是来源事件/消息的新 authority。未修改生产 handler、服务、仓库、配置、API/Proto、Web、迁移或 runner/gate/relocation baseline；schema75、request-binding minimum71 不变。
- 原始 deterministic 命令 `go test -race ./internal/handler/http -run '^TestEventsHandler_Stream_Replay$' -count=1 -timeout=2m` 在未改测试时复现 DATA RACE（`increment19-regression-red.log`，status1）：handler 写 `httptest.ResponseRecorder.Body` 时测试读取 `Body.String()`。这是本轮实测，保留 Increment18 原失败，不以之后 scoped PASS 擦除历史。
- 仅修改测试 harness：handler 独占 recorder，Flush 通过不缓冲 channel 发布不可变 string snapshot；测试不再并发读 body/header/status/fetcher。订阅后设置 SSE header 的 handler 内观察点提供就绪握手；无效 cursor 用随后真实 Hub live marker 的 flush 证明 replay 分支已结束，不再 sleep 猜测。等待保持原2s上限，取消可释放无人接收的 Flush，正常/失败清理都 cancel + join，之后才断言可变状态；专门新增无人读取 flush 的取消回归，不靠扩大 timeout、轮询或 skip。
- 原 replay 顺序、两条完整 JSON event/data、唯一 fetch、actor、原 millis cursor、limit50、live 在 replay 后和取消返回/HTTP200 断言保留；live 必须严格追加一次，退出后的最终 body 也必须一致。补 header/query 两条入口、有效 header 优先于 query、无效 header 不借有效 query、各入口 malformed/zero/negative cursor 回归。没有增加生产就绪 hook、网络依赖、JWT、权限或“gap-free SSE”承诺；README 给出 Docker-free race 命令及 best-effort/部署验收边界。

## Increment 32 — 前台任务详情读取不再被自动 GET 抢占

- 完整读取本轮 `AGENTS.md` 和 tracker，独立核对21交接 `handoff/closeout.json`、原始专项/backend/Web/race/格式回执，以及31报告与原始门禁。上一轮 `after-manifest.json`、本轮只读 `before-manifest.json` 与当前2915个 Git 可见条目完全一致，初始差异0，13个既有删除保留（`increment32-handoff-check.json`）。21–31既有实现、原模型失败/无有效第4轮报告及历史门禁失败未重写、恢复、盲重放或算作本批新写。
- 核对实际 composition-root、HTTP GET、原 client/transport、详情和读取/polling hooks：轮询原暂停条件没有前台 `resource.loading`，手动读取超过原自动间隔就会被新的后台 GET abort/取代。先补 real client 接入两个详情的 deterministic 回归，原样26项实测24失败/2通过（`increment32-regression-red.log`）；除抓到新的 GET/取消外，还实测手动403/503/错目标/陈旧回应被旧状态的自动读取掩盖。该证据只证明本地协调/展示缺口，不冒称后端绕权、生产响应错误、远端效果或审计失败。

### 调度成本修正 — 只让可执行增量进入付费任务

- 2026-10-20用户明确要求避免重复盘点与阻塞项浪费。33项不是本轮交付：14 covered对应已有登录/导航、概览/资源、来源/成员、Profile、任务/Operation、下载、恢复和审计导航；19 blocked混合了7项合同/owner待核、5项本轮范围或持久化扩展、7项真实环境/发布验收前提。它们不能作为40个待实施功能或33个永久不可做结论。
- 特别区分开发前提与发布接受：客户端合同已描述IM通知和Vault租户摘要，不应把“尚未核实字段级来源语义/真实版本”说成“没有任何合同”，也不能仅因live未验收就禁止批准合同范围内的本地实现。D09/D10需定点核合同，而非全面重规划或自动假定可用。
- 核原始账本：19次调用中12次探针、5次全量方向分析、仅2次启动实施，后者无交付。重复恢复时未复用已知信息，格式问题又触发重新分析，预检也未覆盖真实shell/Chrome路径；这是调度设计与接入问题，不是业务工作完成。原调用与失败记录不删除、不追认绿、不重置预算；成本unknown。
- 已接受方向目录只盘点一次，恢复读取原结果。将7个候选、14个已覆盖、19个阻塞分开；只给执行者当前选中任务及必要依赖，不把40项/全部历史反复塞入提示。已覆盖只随相关代码/合同变化或反例复核；阻塞只随对应证据/owner/环境变化复核。不机械凑40个新方向或恰好两个worker；最多2并发，只有1个可做就1个，没有就停止。
- 固定协议模板先离线校验。格式错误保原输出并停机，不再另开run、重做40项或重花两次探针；确有需求/相关证据变化才做一次增量计划。不自动重试同一失败任务，不用放宽validator/门禁解决语义错误。例行凭据元数据/实际工具预检不调用模型，同配置不重复付费探针；每次实际任务仍核provider/model/max的真实session绑定，绝不把缓存当当前认证/资源授权。
- 新离线队列/准入材料：`/home/u1/.abr/projects/aero-id-eec51e0cd2e85cf3/runs/dispatch-admission-review-20261020T011218Z/`，含 `candidate-queue.json`、`excluded-register.json`、`policy.json`、只读 `admission.py` 及确定性测试。D01/D02复用原精确分配，其他5项无精确分配前不派工；hash只是证据文件变更提示，不代替依赖审查、scope校验或业务授权。当前检查拒绝D01/D02：无新窗口，候选shell修复尚非实际生效adapter，D02浏览器仍失败。
- 本次零新增runner/model请求、无后台启动；10项离线检查通过，但不是新业务功能或产品门禁通过。旧run、runner源码及其控制面未修改，准入材料未接入已停止的旧控制器；未来启动器必须显式消费该队列并把非0准入结果当作禁止调用，不能声称现在已有运行中控制器自动执行这些新规则。修改本节前另存完整Git可见基线；本次只改跟踪文档，不提交/推送/部署。
